==========================================================================================
HRS 2004 Preliminary Imputation File, Version 1
ODBC Reference: [HRS2004a]
Perl script: CodebkC1.pl v1.00 Mon Oct 24 12:04:21 2005

Note to Users: This codebook describes the imputation data for the Health and 
Retirement Study 2004 (HRS2004 Early). If you find any problems with the data, 
please report them to us at hrsquest@isr.umich.edu. 

Printing recommendation: Set margins (left/right/top/bottom) to .5"; print in 
portrait orientation using a mono-space 10-point font.

Note to Users: Before merging the imputation data with HRS 2004 core data, you'll 
need drop from the core data eight respondents who do not fit the requirements of 
the HRS 2004 sample. (See HRS 2004Early Core Release Data Description, 
http://hrsonline.isr.umich.edu/meta/2004/core/desc/h04dd.pdf (p. 12), for more 
detail. The HHIDs and PNs of the eight respondents to be dropped are: 502333 010, 
500075 010, 501383 010, 501679 010, 502587 010, 501851 010, 501353 010, and 501321 010. 

Note to Users: For each variable to be imputed, we generate three imputation- 
related variables: the 'X','C', and 'T' variables. In some cases, when data   
conversion is needed, we also create a 'Z' variable. The other two variables 
used to be in the imputation data, the 'D' and 'E' variables, are no longer 
necessary as they are now part of the HRS core data. For dicussions of our 
imputation strategy as well as the definitions of all the imputation-related 
variables, please check the documation file,'IMPUTE: A SAS Application System for
Missing Value Imputations' at http://hrsonline.isr.umich.edu/docs/userg/dr-007.pdf. 

Note to Users: For variables in Section UH (Asset Reconciliation), when a 'T' variable 
is equal to 1 ('Legitimate missing'), the corresponding asset variable has no error. 
==========================================================================================
